   private final InternalMatcher internalMatcher;\n69. \n70.     JsonAssert(Path path, Configuration configuration, Object actual) {\n71.         super(JsonUtils.convertToJson(actual, \"actual\"), JsonAssert.class);\n72.         this.path = path;\n73.         this.configuration = configuration;\n74.         this.internalMatcher = new InternalMatcher(actual, path.asPrefix(), \"\", configuration);\n75.         usingComparator(new JsonComparator(configuration, path, false));\n76.     }\n77. \n78.     JsonAssert(Object actual, Configuration configuration) {\n79.         this(Path.create(\"\", getPathPrefix(actual)), configuration, actual);\n80.     }\n81. \n82.     /**\n83.      * Moves comparison to given node. Second call navigates from the last position in the JSON.\n84.      */\n85.     @NotNull\n86.     public JsonAssert node(@NotNull String node) {\n87.         return new JsonAssert(path.to(node), configuration, getNode(actual, node));\n88.     }\n89. \n90. \n91.     /**\n92.      * Allows to do multiple comparisons on a document like\n93.      *\n94.      * <code>\n95.      *     assertThatJson(\"{\\\"test\\\":{\\\"a\\\":1, \\\"b\\\":2, \\\"c\\\":3}}\").and(\n96.      *         a -&gt; a.node(\"test\").isObject(),\n97.      *         a -&gt; a.node(\"test.b\").isEqualTo(3)\n98.      *     );\n99.      * </code>\n100.      */\n101.     @NotNull\n102.     public JsonAssert and(@NotNull JsonAssertion... assertions) {\n103.         Arrays.stream(assertions).forEach(a -> a.doAssert(this));\n104.         return this;\n105.     }\n106. \n107.     /**\n108.      * Compares JSONs.\n109.      */\n110.     @Override\n111.     @NotNull\n112.     public JsonAssert isEqualTo(@Nullable Object expected) {\n113.         Diff diff = Diff.create(expected, actual, \"fullJson\", path.asPrefix(), configuration);\n114. \n115.         String overridingErrorMessage = info.overridingErrorMessage();\n116.         if (!isNullOrEmpty(overridingErrorMessage) && !diff.similar()) {\n117.             failWithMessage(overridingErrorMessage);\n118.         } else {\n119.             diff.failIfDifferent(MessageFormatter.instance().format(info.description(), info.representation(), \"\"));\n120.         }\n121.         return this;\n122.     }\n123. \n124.     /**\n125.      * Copy from AssertJ to prevent errors with percents in error message\n126.      */\n127.     private void failWithMessage(String errorMessage) {\n128.       AssertionError assertionError = Failures.instance().failureIfErrorMessageIsOverridden(info);\n129.       if (assertionError == null) {\n130.         // error message was not overridden, build it.\n131.         String description = MessageFormatter.instance().format(info.description(), info.representation(), \"\");\n132.         assertionError = new AssertionError(description + errorMessage);\n133.       }\n134.       Failures.instance().removeAssertJRelatedElementsFromStackTraceIfNeeded(assertionError);\n135.       throw assertionError;\n136.     }\n137. \n138.     /**\n139.      * Asserts that given node is present and is of type object.\n140.      *\n141.      * @return MapAssert where the object is serialized as Map\n142.      */\n143.     @NotNull\n144.     @SuppressWarnings(\"unchecked\")\n145.     public MapAssert<String, Object> isObject() {\n146.         Node node = assertType(OBJECT);\n147.         return new JsonMapAssert((Map<String, Object>) node.getValue(), path.asPrefix(), configuration)\n148.             .as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n149.     }\n150. \n151.     /**\n152.      * Asserts that given node is present and is of type number.\n153.      */\n154.     @NotNull\n155.     public BigDecimalAssert isNumber() {\n156.         Node node = assertType(NUMBER);\n157.         return createBigDecimalAssert(node.decimalValue());\n158.     }\n159. \n160.     /**\n161.      * Asserts that the value is an integer. 1 is an integer 1.0, 1.1, 1e3, 1e0, 1e-3 is not.\n162.      */\n163.     public BigIntegerAssert isIntegralNumber() {\n164.         Node node = internalMatcher.assertIntegralNumber();\n165.         return new BigIntegerAssert(node.decimalValue().toBigIntegerExact())\n166.             .as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n167.     }\n168. \n169.     /**\n170.      * Asserts that given node is present and is of type number or a string that can be parsed as a number.\n171.      */\n172.     @NotNull\n173.     public BigDecimalAssert asNumber() {\n174.         internalMatcher.isPresent(NUMBER.getDescription());\n175.         Node node = getNode(actual, \"\");\n176.         if (node.getNodeType() == NUMBER) {\n177.             return createBigDecimalAssert(node.decimalValue());\n178.         } else if (node.getNodeType() == STRING) {\n179.             try {\n180.                 return createBigDecimalAssert(new BigDecimal(node.asText()));\n181.             } catch (NumberFormatException e) {\n182.                 failWithMessage(\"Node \\\"\" + path + \"\\\" can not be converted to number expected: <a number> but was: <\" + quoteTextValue(node.getValue()) + \">.\");\n183.             }\n184.         } else {\n185.             internalMatcher.failOnType(node, \"number or string\");\n186.         }\n187.         return null;\n188.     }\n189. \n190.     private BigDecimalAssert createBigDecimalAssert(BigDecimal value) {\n191.         return new BigDecimalAssert(value).as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n192.     }\n193. \n194.     /**\n195.      * Asserts that given node is present and is of type array.\n196.      *\n197.      * @return\n198.      */\n199.     @NotNull\n200.     public ListAssert<Object> isArray() {\n201.         Node node = assertType(ARRAY);\n202.         return new JsonListAssert((List<?>)node.getValue(), path.asPrefix(), configuration)\n203.             .as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n204.     }\n205. \n206.     /**\n207.      * Asserts that given node is present and is of type boolean.\n208.      *\n209.      * @return\n210.      */\n211.     @NotNull\n212.     public BooleanAssert isBoolean() {\n213.         Node node = assertType(BOOLEAN);\n214.         return new BooleanAssert((Boolean) node.getValue()).as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n215.     }\n216. \n217.     /**\n218.      * Asserts that given node is present and is of type string.\n219.      *\n220.      * @return\n221.      */\n222.     @NotNull\n223.     public StringAssert isString() {\n224.         Node node = assertType(STRING);\n225.         return new StringAssert((String) node.getValue()).as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n226.     }\n227. \n228.     @Override\n229.     @NotNull\n230.     public AbstractStringAssert<?> asString() {\n231.         return isString();\n232.     }\n233. \n234.     /**\n235.      * Asserts that given node is present and is null.\n236.      *\n237.      * @return\n238.      */\n239.     @Override\n240.     public void isNull() {\n241.         assertType(NULL);\n242.     }\n243. \n244.     /**\n245.      * Asserts that given node is present and is URI.\n246.      *\n247.      * @return\n248.      */\n249.     @NotNull\n250.     public UriAssert isUri() {\n251.         Node node = assertType(STRING);\n252.         return new UriAssert(URI.create((String) node.getValue())).as(\"Different value found in node \\\"%s\\\"\", path);\n253.     }\n254. \n255.     /**\n256.      * Asserts that given node is present.\n257.      */\n258.     @NotNull\n259.     public JsonAssert isPresent() {\n260.         internalMatcher.isPresent();\n261.         return this;\n262.     }\n263. \n264.     /**\n265.      * Asserts that given node is absent.\n266.      */\n267.     public void isAbsent() {\n268.         internalMatcher.isAbsent();\n269.     }\n270. \n271.     /**\n272.      * Asserts that given node is present and is not null.\n273.      */\n274.     @Override\n275.     @NotNull\n276.     public JsonAssert isNotNull() {\n277.         internalMatcher.isNotNull();\n278.         return this;\n279.     }\n280. \n281.     private Node assertType(Node.NodeType type) {\n282.         return internalMatcher.assertType(type);\n283.     }\n284. \n285.     /**\n286.      * JsonAssert that can be configured to prevent mistakes like\n287.      *\n288.      * <code>\n289.      * assertThatJson(...).isEqualsTo(...).when(...);\n290.      * </code>\n291.      */\n292.     public static class ConfigurableJsonAssert extends JsonAssert {\n293.         // Want to pass to inPath to not parse twice.\n294.         private final Object originalActual;\n295. \n296.
